Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [139] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2 提出了一种高效的长期记忆框架，用语义段落级整合替代逐轮对话整合，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低记忆构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM代理的记忆系统依赖逐轮交互后的即时整合（eager consolidation），随着对话增长成本越来越高；粗粒度摘要又会丢失细粒度证据，而扩大检索上下文或进行多跳推理则把开销转移到查询阶段。

Method: 将多个交互批量编码为段落，利用语义边界检测对每个已完成的段落编码为上下文无关的带类型记忆记录；段落级批处理降低LLM编码频率，之后再组织轻量级结构化索引支持查询规划的证据检索。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1-Mini在LoCoMo上达到89.22%，在LongMemEval-S上达到92.20%；与A-Mem相比，构建token在LoCoMo上减少86.0%，在LongMemEval-S上减少75.9%，且不增加查询时token消耗。

Conclusion: 长期代理记忆的准确率-成本权衡不仅取决于保留什么信息，还取决于信息整合的粒度；段落级整合是一种有效的设计方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [140] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridRAG-BN，一个面向孟加拉语知识库问答的检索增强框架，结合BM25和BGE-M3混合检索、Gemma模型生成答案、LoRA微调模型验证答案，以及后处理阶段处理未解决案例，在公共和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的F1分数，获得比赛第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的KBQA系统面临检索研究匮乏、语言资源稀缺、以及生成响应难以基于外部知识进行接地等挑战，亟需有效的检索增强框架来解决这些问题。

Method: HybridRAG-BN框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 使用BM25和BGE-M3进行混合检索；(2) 使用GGUF版本的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct生成答案；(3) 使用LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct进行答案验证和精炼；(4) 后处理阶段通过回退答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索应对未解决案例。

Result: 在公共和私有排行榜上分别取得token级F1分数0.71654和0.72912，在比赛中获得第一名。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明提出的HybridRAG-BN框架在孟加拉语KBQA任务上表现优异，证明了混合检索与多阶段生成验证机制在低资源语言问答场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [141] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EviReform方法，通过将检索请求的修订与图证据聚合分离，利用已检索到的源段落生成残差查询，从而在多跳问答中引导图检索补充原问题未明确的部分证据。在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，Recall@5最高提升5.59点，F1最高提升4.50点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能共同提供充分证据的段落，但初始段落往往解析了问题中隐含的实体或关系，使得缺失证据在检索开始后才更容易描述。现有图检索的检索信号通常来自原始问题，导致互补证据必须通过存储关系才能到达，即使已观察到的段落提供了更直接的语义线索。

Method: EviReform将修订检索请求与在图中的证据聚合分开处理。具体而言，检索到的源段落为未解决的信息需求构建残差查询；原始的与残差的检索信号分别进行归一化处理后组合，并在共享实体的命题之间传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个基准上，EviReform相比最强基线在Recall@5上最高提升5.59点，在F1上最高提升4.50点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，已观察到的证据可以引导图检索朝着支持链中原问题未充分指定的部分进行搜索，验证了利用检索过程中的中间证据来改进多跳检索的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [142] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve是一个无需可信参考或毒化标签的RAG投毒检测框架，通过查询端（RSQ）与语料端（RSG）的自我参照对比，分别检测检索排序中的答案锚点集中和语料中的协同密度异常，联合部署可将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG投毒检测器依赖可信外部参考、特定攻击伪影或对语料拓扑敏感的全局阈值，无法在无需先验知识的场景下有效检测注入文档对检索结果的操控。

Method: 提出RAGSieve框架，包含两个互补模块：RAGSieve-Query（RSQ）通过查询局部对比，利用同一检索中排名1-5与排名6-20的得分差异检测答案锚点集中与载体转换；RAGSieve-Graph（RSG）通过语料局部对比，比较每个文档的语义相近但词汇不同的邻居与其局部基线，在查询到来前检测协同密度异常。

Result: 在三个问答数据集和六种投毒构建下，RSQ达到95.2% AUROC并在删除5%干净文档时检测出82.2%的投毒（对比GMTP的81.1%/52.5%）；RSG达到93.3%/79.8%（对比CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%）；联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%，同时在未投毒检索上保持41.3%的F1。

Conclusion: RAGSieve通过构建系统自身的参照基准，在语料摄取与查询两个阶段提供无需毒化标签或可信语料的实用保护，兼具高检测率与良好的正常检索保持能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [143] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA，一种结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B的更简单架构，在自动口语评估领域取得最先进性能（RMSE 0.358），同时提供更可解释的语音表达与内容分离，并通过消融实验和重复运行分析声学与内容信息的贡献及性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态语音大语言模型的自动口语评估研究缺乏对声学信息和内容信息各自贡献的深入分析，也缺乏对模型性能稳定性的评估。

Method: 提出CASA架构，结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B，采用约一半的推理参数，依赖三个手工设计的流利度特征，采用通用架构设计，可适配其他ASA语料库而无需结构改动。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上取得RMSE 0.358，超越此前最优结果，同时推理参数减少约一半。通过消融实验分析了声学与内容信息的独立及互补贡献，通过重复运行评估了性能变异性，并展示了LLM推理用于免训练内容验证的潜力。

Conclusion: CASA证明更简单的架构可以在提升性能的同时增强可解释性，声学与内容信息的分离分析有助于理解模型预测机制，LLM推理可有效用于训练无关的内容验证。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [144] [LigBench: A Unified and Human-Aligned Benchmark for LLM-based Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13136)
*Chenrun Wang,Mingxuan Zhu,Tiancheng Huang,Wenjie Li,Yujie Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Zhiying Zou,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LigBench，一个自动化评估基准，用于对AI研究思路进行细粒度且可靠的评估，并配套提出PAIR-IQ数据集用于训练成对思路判断模型，两者结合为研究思路生成提供客观、统一的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明思路生成方法日益受到关注，但评估实践缺乏统一标准，往往依赖LLM直接打分，难以在生成思路的分布上提供一致可靠的评价。

Method: 提出LigBench自动化评估基准，支持跨不同生成分布的细粒度评估；同时构建PAIR-IQ数据集，用于训练成对思路判断模型，作为辅助参考以增强客观比较。

Result: 实验表明LigBench评估稳定且可解释，与专家判断的一致性显著提升；基于PAIR-IQ训练的模型在排序准确性和鲁棒性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: LigBench与PAIR-IQ共同建立了可扩展且客观的研究思路评估原则性标准，为研究思路生成领域提供了更加可靠的评估范式。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), research idea generation has attracted increasing attention. Existing approaches enable LLMs to retrieve relevant literature and propose novel ideas for research areas. However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [145] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syfer是一个用于多语言多跳问答的合成器-折叠框架，采用延迟翻译而非默认翻译的策略，先进行格式约束的子问题分解和质量检查，仅在必要时才激活翻译通路，在多语言实验中取得了有竞争力的准确率并兼顾了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG方法存在两个局限：一是统一翻译会丢弃目标语言特有的文化和语言信息、引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；二是贪婪的子问题分解和聚合会产生冗余子问题，逐步推理中错误被放大。

Method: Syfer框架首先调用格式约束的分解器生成原始语言的子问题图，然后进行分解质量检查；检查通过时，按检索-回答策略在目标语言中顺序回答子问题；检查失败时才激活带双语子问题图对齐的英文翻译通路。

Result: 跨多种语言的实验表明，Syfer在保持有竞争力准确率的同时，实现了性能与计算成本之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译策略通过按需激活翻译通路，避免了默认翻译带来的信息损失和成本开销，为多语言多跳问答提供了一种更高效且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [146] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECBench基准，将成人依恋理论引入LLM评估，用ECR-R量表刻画模型的依恋焦虑与回避倾向，在四个场景（情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交引导）和两类关系（友谊、浪漫）中评估32个LLM的情感陪伴能力，涵盖11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于情感陪伴，但现有评估主要关注一般人格特质，缺乏对模型在亲密关系和情感敏感场景中行为的洞察。作者引入成人依恋理论，为LLM情感陪伴能力提供心理学视角的评估框架。

Method: 构建ECBench基准，包含四个场景（情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交引导）及两类关系类型；使用ECR-R量表量化模型的依恋焦虑和回避倾向；通过11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法，对32个LLM进行全面评估，并选取代表性模型进行多轮上下文交互分析及提示词引导下的行为塑造实验。

Result: 评估了32个LLM的依恋倾向，发现不同模型在依恋焦虑和回避维度上存在显著差异；代表性模型在情境化多轮交互中展现出与依恋倾向一致的行为模式，且通过提示词可以在一定程度上塑造模型的依恋相关行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和选择适合情感陪伴场景的LLM提供了心理学理论视角与实践评估工具，弥补了现有评估在亲密情感场景中的不足，对情感陪伴型LLM的开发与选型具有指导意义。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [147] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GEM，一种生成式嵌入模型，通过显式推理用户意图和相关性标准来增强检索。GEM统一了生成与嵌入，先对查询进行推理，再附加嵌入token编码增强后的上下文。实验表明GEM在推理密集型和指令遵循型检索任务上优于非推理变体，并能通过提示实现测试时计算扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索器主要依赖查询与文档之间的表面匹配，而现代LLM用户能够表达复杂多样的信息需求，这导致用户表达需求的方式与检索器理解需求的方式之间日益脱节。

Method: GEM将生成与嵌入统一在单一模型中：首先对查询进行显式推理（理解用户意图和相关性标准），然后附加嵌入token对推理后的增强上下文进行编码，用于检索。

Result: 在推理密集型和指令遵循型检索任务上，GEM优于其非推理变体，并匹配了使用更大模型的基线。此外，GEM的生成特性允许通过提示实现测试时计算扩展，进一步提升检索性能。

Conclusion: 推理增强的检索策略是有效的，GEM证明了在检索前对用户意图进行显式推理能够显著提升检索质量，且通过生成式设计获得的测试时计算扩展能力为进一步性能提升提供了可能。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VLSR框架，通过"先定位后推理"策略，让LLM在分子图像上先定位化学相关区域，再在潜在工作空间中进行属性推理，相比文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的化学推理方法要么接收SMILES/分子图像加局部基序描述，要么直接从分子图像推理，都无法让模型在推理前聚焦于化学上有意义的区域，缺乏显式的局部化学感知与属性推理结合机制。

Method: 提出Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR)，一个端到端框架，采用"先定位后推理"(localize-then-reason)策略：首先学习在分子图像中定位化学上有意义的区域，然后在紧凑的潜在工作空间中推理这些区域的属性影响，最后生成最终答案。

Result: 在相同的推理设置下，VLSR相比可比较的文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习定位与推理，VLSR能够使模型在推理前聚焦于化学关键区域，并在保证推理能力的同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次量化了自我指涉提示引发的LLM"主观体验报告"在重复独立试验中的不稳定性，发现其不稳定性（0.343）显著高于不可解决的哲学问题（0.192）和有可验证正确答案的问题（0.105），表明这类报告在模型输出分布中占据独特且更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明自我指涉提示能可靠地诱导LLM产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但没有任何先前工作测量这些报告在重复独立试验中的一致性，也没有将其与其他类型的开放式问题进行比较。缺乏定量基线来理解这类报告在模型输出空间中的位置。

Method: 定义响应不稳定性指标（1减去从每个响应提取的核心主张的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度），对比三类问题：自我指涉提示（引发主观体验报告）、不可解决的哲学问题（与自我指涉无关）、有可验证正确答案的问题。每组4个问题，每题30次独立响应（共360个响应，Gemini API，温度0.7）。

Result: 自我指涉问题不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），不可解决的哲学问题居中且紧密聚集（0.192±0.008），可验证问题最低（0.105±0.058）。三类问题间存在显著差异，且自我指涉问题的不稳定性标准差也最大。

Conclusion: 自我指涉引发的"主观体验报告"在模型输出分布中占据一个与普通开放式哲学不确定性截然不同的、稳定性更差的位置。这为诱导主观体验报告提供了定量基线，表明其独特性可通过输出分布的不稳定性加以区分。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigBench，一个用于科学图表理解的诊断性VLM基准，联合评估感知、推理和不确定性下的行为可靠性，并提出A-R-I框架度量模型是否承认证据不足、抵抗误导性上下文、从部分信息中谨慎推断。实验发现GPT-5.2和Gemini 3.1 Pro在感知与推理上相当，但行为可靠性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准过度聚焦于感知和推理准确性，忽视了模型在视觉证据缺失或误导时的不确定性行为可靠性，这在实际科学工作流部署中至关重要。

Method: 构建包含250幅科学图表的SciFigBench基准，通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、标题偏见探针和选择性模糊目标生成34,000+测试场景；提出Admittance-Resistance-Inductance（A-R-I）三维度框架评估模型行为可靠性。

Result: GPT-5.2达到最高描述质量（MQM 91.6）和较高推理准确率（78.4%），但在96%情况下幻觉不可读内容；Gemini 3.1 Pro在相当能力水平下（MQM 90.2，推理81.0%）71%情况下承认不确定性，抵抗得分达0.91。高感知和推理准确率不保证行为可靠性。

Conclusion: 感知和推理的高准确率并不等同于行为可靠性，不确定性下的行为维度对于科学工作流中的VLM部署具有关键意义，未来基准需将行为可靠性作为独立评估维度。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture of Training (MoT)，一种模块化预训练方法，将目标Transformer划分为连续层块，分别在冻结的aligner脚手架中独立训练，再重组为完整模型。在1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型上验证了可行性，达到与单块训练相当的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练能否分解为更小的、可独立训练的单元，这些单元之后可以重新组合成一个连贯的大型模型，从而研究训练的可复用性和模块化。

Method: MoT将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块，每个块在冻结的预训练aligner脚手架中独立训练，训练完成后将各块重组，并可选择进行简短的自适应端到端微调。

Result: 在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型（C4数据集）上，独立训练的深度切片可以重组为可用的语言模型，质量对等的调度方案达到与单块基线相同的困惑度。该方案处理更多聚合token，但具有更短的理想化层等效关键路径。

Conclusion: MoT并非通用替代单块预训练的方法，而是作为一个小规模框架，用于研究脚手架化的子训练能否作为可复用的训练单元；其有效计算优势依赖于跨运行复用aligner。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WIFA方法，通过自动意图组增强（将包装的有害样本与结构匹配的良性反例配对）来改善安全微调中的表面形式捷径问题。两种微调路线：WIFA-Boost提升有害拒绝率，A-GCRT降低过度拒绝率（Qwen上从25.7%降至17.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调虽然能提升有害内容拒绝能力，但模型可能学习表面形式的捷径——包装过的有害提示能绕过安全机制，而包装过的良性提示却被过度拒绝。这暴露了安全微调在泛化上的缺陷。

Method: 提出Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA)，一种自动意图组增强方法，将包装的有害样本与结构匹配的包装良性反例配对，无需外部教师模型或人工标注包装意图。基于WIFA构建两种互补微调路线：WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全配方）和Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT)（在同一意图的包装间正则化拒绝/遵从决策分数，将有害与良性组锚定在间隔两侧）。

Result: Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost达到最强的变换后有害拒绝性能；A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线模型的25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线方法未能达到这些操作点。Llama实验和消融研究支持意图组解释。

Conclusion: WIFA及其两条微调路线有效缓解了安全微调中因表面形式捷径导致的绕过与过度拒绝问题。实验与消融结果支持意图组解释视角，但作者不声称在所有基线上都能实现低于基线的过度拒绝率。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协议级别的可识别性审计方法，在受控、求解器约束的场景下，通过有限行为策略类来检验观测协议是否能够区分具有不同目标量（estimand）的策略，并且该方法无需任何模型调用即可完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试的分数可能在被测行为属性无法被观测协议识别的情况下依然显得精确，即观测协议可能无法区分行为属性，导致评估结果失真。作者希望在设计评估协议时，在模型推理之前就结构性验证评估设计的有效性。

Method: 形式化定义协议级可识别性审计：给定策略集H、观测支持集O和目标量τ，测试O是否能分离所有具有不同τ的策略对。该方法零模型调用。实验采用冻结的确定性策略，比较基准观测与完整支持、逐次留一支持等不同观测条件下的分类结果，并引入一个基线约束生成变体进行经验验证。

Result: 基准观测将七个冻结确定性策略压缩为一个等价类；完整支持产生七个类别且无跨目标量碰撞；每个留一支持都保留了构造性碰撞证据。经验上，两个约束生成变体对的有效性均为1.0，但基准准确率与选择性响应保真度存在显著差异（0.620 vs 0.324，簇引导95% CI不重叠），并在第二个确定性来源上重复出现（0.646 vs 0.331）。审计还综合出最小识别支持集O*，仅需两个单元而非完整的36单元张量。

Conclusion: 评估设计的有效性可以在模型推理之前通过结构化方式检查，且基线正确性并不能决定干预响应的保真度。该审计方法为基准测试协议设计提供了一种理论驱动的验证手段。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM对含有女性常用语言特征（如模糊限制语、反意疑问句、集体指称）的提示会生成更短、更不复杂、更不正式的回答。该效应在三种文档类型和四种模型中一致存在，且比显性性别线索（如署名）的影响更大。事后缓解困难，因为语言特征嵌入在Transformer早期层且与语义纠缠，建议在上游设计时考虑语言变异的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地介入职业沟通，需要审视这些模型是否对所有用户平等服务。已有研究多关注显性性别线索，但忽视了日常语言中隐含的性别相关语言特征可能带来的系统性偏差。

Method: 在四种LLM和三种文档类型上，构造包含女性常用语言特征（模糊限制语、反意疑问句、集体指称）的提示，与基线提示进行对照。控制提示复杂度和特征延续效应后比较输出长度、复杂度和正式程度。同时进行表征空间分析和机制层面的层归因分析。

Result: 女性常用语言特征系统性引发更短、更不复杂、更不正式的输出；效应跨模型和文档类型一致。显性性别线索（署名姓名）被编码在与语言方言相同的表征空间，但对输出的实际影响很小。语言特征是文化嵌入的、不在用户意识控制之内，事后规避困难；机制分析显示特征编码于Transformer早期层且与其他特征纠缠。

Conclusion: LLM对语言变异的系统性处理会放大职场沟通中的不平等。由于事后缓解困难，需要在模型开发的上游环节就考虑语言多样性的影响，以减少LLM中介职场沟通中的差异性影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RippleMem，一种面向LLM智能体的长期记忆系统，通过自适应联想回忆替代一次性检索，以事件为中心的语义图和线索丰富的记忆单元来恢复分散的证据。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上取得最优性能，分别提升3.95%和11.87%的准确率，同时将图构建成本降低约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的外部记忆核心瓶颈不在于存储，而在于当相关信息分散在多次交互中时如何恢复正确的证据集。现有方法各有缺陷：全上下文方法需要嘈杂的长上下文搜索，平面检索常返回孤立不完整的记录，图记忆系统构建昂贵且压缩了丰富的事件上下文。

Method: RippleMem将交互历史存储为线索丰富的记忆单元（cue-rich episodic memory units），并组织成事件中心记忆图。查询时首先通过混合线索召回相关记忆锚点，然后沿着语义和结构关联从锚点扩展，以恢复缺失的支持性证据。初始召回的记忆不仅作为回答上下文，还作为完成证据收集的线索。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，RippleMem在所有评估设置中取得最佳整体性能：LLM-as-a-Judge准确率在LoCoMo上提升3.95%，在LongMemEval-S上最高提升11.87%，同时图构建成本降低约30倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem验证了借鉴线索依赖情景记忆和联想完成机制的联想式回忆方法，能够有效解决长时程推理中分散证据的恢复问题，兼具高性能与低成本优势。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP方法，通过释义校准的反事实敏感性边际来量化任务相关性，实现选择性在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的token级监督分配，相较于最强非CROP选择器在两种师生设置下分别提升1.92和2.96个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）对所有响应token赋予平等的监督权重，而现有的选择性OPD方法主要关注优化需求（如不确定性或师生分歧），对任务相关性（监督是否与当前输入的语义内容紧密关联）缺乏直接刻画。作者旨在填补这一维度空白。

Method: CROP通过三个关键步骤实现：(1) 为每个源提示构建经过验证的原始-释义-反事实三元组；(2) 保持学生模型rollout固定，测量每个响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感性；(3) 使用对保持语义的改写（释义）的敏感性进行校准，从而得到反事实敏感性边际作为任务相关性分数，并据此非均匀地分配token级监督。

Result: 匹配选择控制实验表明，CROP识别出比随机或最低相关性选择更有用的监督位置；组件比较证实了反事实敏感性和释义校准两者的价值。在两个师生设置下，CROP比最强非CROP选择器分别提高1.92和2.96个百分点的聚合性能。

Conclusion: 任务相关性可作为选择性OPD的补充标准，CROP作为一种模型内部、对比特定的方法，能够有效用于token级监督分配，为选择性蒸馏提供了新的维度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过层析分析九种SSL语音骨干网络，在多种跨语料场景下评估帕金森病检测的泛化能力，发现最优特征层高度依赖语料来源而非模型架构，且模型学到的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，无法区分帕金森病与痴呆症。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音表征在单个语料库内检测帕金森病表现优异，但尚不清楚模型真正捕获的是疾病相关特征还是数据集特定混淆因素，尤其是大多数SSL骨干仅用健康语音预训练，其跨语料泛化能力值得深入探究。

Method: 对九个SSL语音骨干网络进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，在三种语言上构建多个逐步引入分布偏移（参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理类型）的评估场景。

Result: 两个关键发现：其一，最优表征层的选择高度依赖语料来源，由源数据集而非SSL架构本身决定；其二，迁移后的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，训练用于检测帕金森病的分类器对目标语料中的帕金森病和痴呆语音都给出了相似的高概率判定。

Conclusion: 当前语音病理识别模型在临床部署前仍存在关键局限，必须解决特征层选择的语料依赖性和判别信号缺乏病理特异性等问题，才能确保模型的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了指令微调语言模型在问答任务中自信度变化与生成理由词汇多样性之间的关系，发现指令微调会显著改变模型自信度，且跨理由的多样性一致下降，但表面层词汇多样性变化不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型在多种生成任务上表现优异，但近期研究表明其会表现出过度自信。在问答任务中，模型的自信度可能与其生成的支持性理由的一致性相关。作者希望探究指令微调所引发的模型自信度变化是否伴随有生成理由词汇多样性的相应变化。

Method: 作者评估了三组匹配的基础模型与指令微调模型，在多个问答基准上进行实验。通过对比指令微调前后的自信度、预测准确率、基于似然的校准度，以及两类词汇多样性指标（跨理由多样性和表面层词汇多样性）来分析指令微调的影响，并控制答案选择和理由长度等因素。

Result: 研究发现：1) 指令微调一致地改变回答自信度，尽管预测准确率变化有限且似然校准度下降；2) 指令微调对理由多样性产生非均匀影响——跨理由多样性一致下降，而表面层词汇多样性在不同模型和基准上方向和幅度各异；3) 控制答案选择和理由长度后，这些差异仍然存在，说明自信度与理由多样性捕捉了指令微调的不同效应。

Conclusion: 指令微调对模型自信度和理由多样性的影响是不同且可区分的，这为理解指令微调如何改变模型行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在面对超出知识边界的实体时，尽管其内部激活已编码了知识边界感知和指称特定性预测信号，但生成时并未将两者协调，导致模型过度偏好特定指称而非退居通用表述，即具备Gricean退让的"基质"但缺乏执行该策略的"策略层"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在面对知识边界外的实体时，会惯例性地编造看似合理的细节而非退守到更安全、更笼统的表述。作者通过Gricean合作原则的视角，将这一失败框架化为不确定性说话者应当沿特定性层级上移（即牺牲信息量换取真实性），并探究LLM是否具备执行这一退让策略的要素。

Method: 作者基于T-REx基准构建了一个改变实体熟悉度和指称特定性的评测基准，对模型提出两个探测问题：（i）模型的激活是否编码了指称落在知识边界内外的信息；（ii）模型是否预判即将生成的指称的特定性。通过分析这两类内部信号与最终生成行为之间的关系来检验信号是否被协调利用。

Result: 两项探测的答案均为"是"——模型激活中确实同时存在知识边界编码信号和指称特定性预期信号，但这两类信号在生成阶段并未被调和。模型即使在实体未知、甚至在被提供正确的通用替代选项时，仍压倒性地偏好生成特定指称。

Conclusion: LLM具备执行Gricean退让所需的底层信号基础，但缺乏将这些信号转化为生成策略的政策机制。作者将这一发现定位为迈向"Gricean对齐"的第一步，即通过训练或引导目标将知识边界意识与生成过程中的指称特定性选择耦合起来。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需下游任务或验证集即可衡量预训练数据影响力的方法。该方法通过计算梯度更新对最终参数平方距离的减少量来定义样本影响力，并利用中间检查点进行估计。应用于Pythia和PolyPythia的18种配置后发现：训练早期文献类数据与最终参数的轨迹对齐更强，而STEM类数据在后期对齐更强，这种定性转变在多种模型配置中保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型预训练中，衡量训练数据影响力存在两个主要困难：一是难以选择能代表模型通用能力的下游任务或验证集作为归因目标；二是依赖中间检查点的任务性能使得跨训练过程的比较变得复杂。现有方法无法提供一种不依赖特定任务选择的、可跨训练阶段比较的数据影响力度量。

Method: 提出一种以最终参数为归因目标的影响力度量方法。具体地，定义样本影响力为该样本的梯度更新所减少的到最终参数的平方距离，并通过中间检查点估计该量，无需重新训练模型。该方法的优势在于不需要预先选择下游任务或验证集。

Result: 在Pythia和PolyPythia套件的18种配置上应用该方法，发现训练过程中影响力数据存在系统性时间变化：训练早期文献类数据与通向最终参数的轨迹更对齐，而STEM类数据在训练后期更对齐。这种定性交叉在多种模型配置中广泛一致。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可操作、轨迹级的数据影响力视角，揭示预训练过程中影响力数据随时间的变化规律，补充了以特定下游任务或验证集为定义的影响力分析，为理解预训练数据动态提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mimir v1是一个基于Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM)架构的10亿参数语言模型，仅使用许可（permissible）后训练数据从头训练，在英语上表现具有竞争力，并在丹麦语上达到新的最先进水平，在20个基准测试中可与更大的前沿模型（如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B）相竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型的开发依赖海量且往往未经许可的数据集，这为致力于开源和道德数据来源的研究人员设置了很高的入门门槛。作者希望降低这一壁垒。

Method: 采用HRM架构，模型参数规模为10亿，使用161个数据集的混合数据进行从头训练，仅使用许可的后训练数据。

Result: 在英语、数学与代码以及丹麦语共20个基准测试中，Mimir v1优于原HRM-Text 1B模型，并与更大的前沿模型（如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B）竞争；在丹麦语上达到了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: Mimir v1证明仅使用许可数据即可训练出具有竞争力的语言模型，模型已开源在Hugging Face上供社区使用。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEVerbalizer框架，通过将稀疏自编码器的解码器方向注入LLM表示并微调下游层，使LLM能够直接生成SAE features的自然语言解释，无需外部行为观察，解决了传统解释方法表面化和计算低效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE特征解释主要依赖外部观测模型行为来推断语义，这种方法既导致解释流于表面（仅基于观察到的行为），又因需要在规模上收集行为证据而计算效率低下。

Method: SAEVerbalizer将SAE解码器方向注入LLM的表示中，并微调LLM的下游层，使其为注入的特征生成自然语言解释。训练完成后，可直接从解码器方向解释SAE特征。同时引入轻量级适配器以跨不同LLM迁移。

Result: 实验表明：该框架的言语化能力可泛化到未见过的特征；能跨不同SAE字典迁移；通过轻量适配器可扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征。干预实验显示，注入多个方向可生成组合语义的解释，反转单个方向则产生相应的语义变化。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer证明SAE特征可以直接从解码器方向进行解释，无需外部行为观测，且具备良好的泛化性和可迁移性，为SAE特征解释提供了一种高效且更深层的新范式。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LITTLECURRICULUM，一个8.8B token的预训练语料库，专门针对美国小学5年级及以下的内容。基于此训练出5B参数的LITTLELEARNER模型，该模型具备基本语言能力，但知识与能力边界清晰且可映射到课程指南。该沙箱环境可用于研究模型在受控范围下的知识获取、表征和使用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在异构的Web级文本语料上训练，导致难以刻画模型对知识的前期暴露情况，从而难以研究模型的知识和技能获取过程。需要一个定义清晰、范围受限的训练环境来解决这一挑战。

Method: 构建LITTLECURRICULUM语料库，严格排除5年级以上教授的概念、事实和词汇；从零开始训练5B参数的LITTLELEARNER模型；通过开放评估验证其语言能力，并通过后训练和上下文学习等注入实验测试其能力边界。

Result: LITTLELEARNER具备足够的语言能力以支持开放式评估，但知识和能力边界清晰可见。后训练和上下文学习可以在现有知识范围内提升能力，但不能突破范围外的能力边界。

Conclusion: LITTLECURRICULUM和LITTLELEARNER构成一个发展受限的沙箱环境，为研究模型如何在明确定义的训练范围内获取、表征和使用数据提供了有价值的受控实验平台。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [SoM-MTM: Synesthesia of Machines (SoM)-Driven Masked Token Model for Cooperative Perception over Packet Loss Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13245)
*Haozhen Li,Rongqing Zhang,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SoM-MTM，一种受掩码图像建模启发的即插即用视觉协同感知范式，通过Swin Transformer和外部路由MoE机制修复丢包信道下的失真特征，提升感知泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于端到端神经网络的视觉协同感知方法受限于特定信道模型、合作模式和感知任务，缺乏通用性，无法满足下一代移动网络中大规模异构CP需求。

Method: 提出Synesthesia of Machines驱动的掩码Token模型（SoM-MTM），基于Swin Transformer架构，结合外部路由MoE机制嵌入掩码先验信息，最大程度修复和增强协同感知中的环境感知特征。

Result: 综合实验表明SoM-MTM能在多种感知任务上一致提升感知性能，尤其在未见场景上展现出强泛化能力，同时保持良好的模型成本和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SoM-MTM作为一种即插即用的通用视觉协同感知范式，融合掩码图像建模与MoE机制，有效解决丢包信道下的特征恢复问题，为智能体AI与通信网络融合下的CP提供了新思路。

Abstract: To support the large-scale and heterogeneous visual cooperative perception (CP) demands in next-generation mobile networks, intelligent and efficient sensory data transmission is a critical challenge. Under the emerging convergence of communication networks and agentic artificial intelligence (AI), existing research emphasizes utilizing end-to-end neural networks to simplify communication modules, which has shown promising potential for CP. However, these studies are still limited to specific channel models, cooperation modes, and perception tasks, failing to fully leverage powerful visual processing approaches to enhance universality. To address this, we propose a Synesthesia of Machines (SoM)-driven Masked Token Model, referred to as SoM-MTM, as a plug-and-play paradigm for generic visual CP. Inspired by masked image modeling methods such as MAE, it possesses great perceptual context learning capabilities to recover distorted features over packet loss channels, thereby improving information carrying efficiency. Building upon Swin Transformer, SoM-MTM further embeds prior masked information through an External Routing MoE mechanism, maximally repairing and enhancing environmental perception features during cooperation. Comprehensive experimental results confirm that SoM-MTM can consistently enhance perception performances on various tasks, especially strong generalization to unseen scenarios, while maintaining favorable model cost and scalability.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [What Makes a Peer? Valuation-Anchored Similarity in Private Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12594)
*Sebastian Frank,Jingrao Lyu,Max Jarmey,Preetha Saha,Mingshu Li,Sweet Kaur,Sola Akinola,Dhagash Mehta*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于集成树监督相似性学习的框架，通过CatBoost模型从私有公司估值中学习公司相似度度量，用于私有市场中的同行公司识别和估值任务，在约27万家公司的数据集上优于传统距离法和文本嵌入法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有市场投资者面临透明度低、披露稀疏、交易不频繁的问题，传统基于静态特征匹配或语义描述的方法难以识别具有经济意义的可比公司，这给估值、尽职调查、投资组合构建和风险管理带来根本性挑战。

Method: 使用CatBoost梯度提升决策树在可观测的私有公司估值上训练模型，从集成树的叶节点共现中导出基于重要性的加权相似度度量，该度量能捕捉共享估值驱动因素，同时适应非线性关系、混合数据类型和私有市场普遍存在的缺失数据。

Result: 在包含约270,000家全球私有公司（其中超过53,000家具有可观测或可推导的投后估值）的数据集上，该框架在多个行业组的下游k近邻估值任务中优于传统的基于距离和基于文本嵌入的方法，并保留基于案例的可解释性。

Conclusion: 估值感知的相似性学习框架能更有效地识别私有市场中的经济可比公司，为估值任务提供更优的k近邻方法，同时保持案例级别的可解释性，适合实际应用。

Abstract: As more investors contemplate private markets and contend with limited transparency, sparse disclosures, and infrequent transactions, identifying economically meaningful peer companies for comparison is a fundamental challenge for valuation, due diligence, portfolio construction, and risk management. We propose an ensemble tree-based supervised similarity learning framework that defines company similarity through the lens of market valuation rather than static feature matching or semantic descriptions. Specifically, we train a CatBoost gradient-boosted decision tree model on observed private company valuations and derive a valuation-aware similarity metric from importance-weighted leaf-node co-occurrences across the ensemble. The similarity metric captures shared valuation drivers while accommodating nonlinear relationships, mixed data types, and pervasive missing data common in private markets. Using a global private-market universe of approximately 270,000 companies, including more than 53,000 firms with observed or derivable post-money valuations spanning multiple industries, geographies, and deal stages, we demonstrate that the proposed similarity framework improves upon traditional distance-based and text-embedding-based approaches in downstream k-nearest-neighbor valuation tasks in the evaluated industry groups, while retaining case-based explainability.

</details>


### [166] [The Price of Permission: Classification Uncertainty in Constrained Capital Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12634)
*Abdulrahman Qadi,Akash Sharma,Francesca Medda*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了伊斯兰教法合规股票筛选所引发的"分类不确定性"及其对投资者基础的影响。通过对1999-2024年间13,188只证券在七套教法规则书下的分类分析，发现筛选规则分歧和边界邻近度可预测屏幕状态转换。官方纳入事件（马来西亚证监会25份名单）在流动性较高的股票中产生显著正收益（10日1.76个百分点，20日2.25个百分点），但所有权和需求压力诊断未识别出唯一边际买家或清晰的因果需求冲击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰教法合规筛选虽提供透明的制度环境，但二值标签只能反映当前资格，无法捕捉投资者基础在不同标准间的分裂程度或接近状态变化的可能性。作者将这种不稳定性定义为"分类不确定性"，旨在量化其对投资者基础的后果，并检验分类风险是否应被纳入投资组合监控。

Method: 采用1999-2024年CRSP-Compustat面板数据，13,188只证券在七套研究者模拟的教法规则书下分类。使用Fama-MacBeth横截面回归检验无条件均衡加权许可溢价，利用DJIM/S&P 2023年9月方法变更作为自然实验。核心事件研究使用马来西亚证监会25份官方名单，构造匹配样本并用[0,10]和[0,20]交易日窗口计算累计异常收益，辅以留一日、首次纳入和中审查安慰剂检验。

Result: 美国市场不支持无条件均衡加权许可溢价；DJIM/S&P方法变更未产生稳健的匹配重定价。马来西亚官方纳入事件中，410只在审查前已交易的纳入股票匹配收益为正但不精确；施加事件前换手率下限后得到295只纳入股票，分别产生10日1.76个百分点（p=0.008）和20日2.25个百分点（p=0.018）的显著收益。安慰剂检验总体支持，但联合20日前事件检验被拒绝。

Conclusion: 证据支持将分类风险视为投资组合监控状态。官方伊斯兰教法许可在公认的本地市场中对充分可交易的证券具有价格效应；仅凭形式上的资格认定不足以保证价格影响。

Abstract: Shariah-compliant equity screening provides a transparent setting in which institutional rules determine who may own a stock. A binary label identifies current eligibility but not whether the feasible investor base is fragmented across standards or close to changing. We define this instability as classification uncertainty and formalize its investor-base consequence through permitted investor mass. In a 1999-2024 CRSP-Compustat panel of 13,188 securities classified under seven researcher-emulated Shariah rulebooks, screening-rule disagreement and proximity to active boundaries rank next-month screen-implied transitions. U.S. Fama-MacBeth diagnostics do not support an unconditional equal-weighted permission premium, and a September 2023 DJIM/S&P methodology change produces no robust matched repricing. The central event evidence uses 25 official Securities Commission Malaysia lists. The 410 inclusions already trading before the preceding review have positive but imprecise matched returns. Applying the pre-event turnover floor yields 295 inclusions with 1.76 percentage points over $[0,10]$ trading days ($p_{\mathrm{date}}=0.008$; $p_{\mathrm{wild}}=0.017$) and 2.25 points over $[0,20]$ ($p_{\mathrm{date}}=0.018$; $p_{\mathrm{wild}}=0.035$). Leave-one-date-out, first-inclusion-only, and mid-review placebo checks are supportive, although a joint 20-day pre-event test rejects. Ownership and demand-pressure diagnostics do not identify a unique marginal buyer or clean causal demand shock. The evidence supports treating classification risk as a portfolio-monitoring state. Official Shariah permission is associated with price effects in a recognized local market among sufficiently tradable securities; formal eligibility alone is insufficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [MASCOT: Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12532)
*Aaryan Sharma,Vishak Prasad C,Virendra Singh,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MASCOT方法，通过将多属性多样性建模为资源分配问题（软分箱+查询驱动加权），替代传统流形排斥的MS-DPP方法，在复合属性（地理+时间）的多样性抑制任务中显著提升了早期排名召回率，例如R@10从67.63%提升至88.58%，但作者声明其优势仅限于复合约束下的排名1之后的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM检索系统仅关注相关性，忽略了结果多样性（RD），尤其在复合属性（如地理和时间）上的精确控制非常困难。MS-DPP虽然通过流形排斥实现广泛探索，但在多样性抑制任务中，基于流形的方法在离散元数据上会导致早期排名召回率严重下降。

Method: MASCOT将多属性多样性视为资源分配问题，将属性投影到软分箱空间并由查询驱动的权重加权，而非依赖流形排斥。该方法将多样性控制转化为覆盖率优化问题，以精确控制复合属性上的多样性增减。

Result: 在PixelProse三个多样性抑制任务中，MASCOT保持了88.58%的R@10，而MS-DPP仅为67.63%。在PP_geo_hour复合约束下，MS-DPP的R@10从0.9737降至0.4931，R@1降至0.23；MASCOT保持R@10=0.9410和R@1=0.7202。但作者承认在聚合多样性-相关性分数上，更简单的消融变体在三个任务上都取得了更高的调和均值。

Conclusion: MASCOT在复合属性约束下展现了对早期排名召回率的显著保护优势，但并非在所有指标上全面优于基线——其优势特定于复合约束下的排名1之后的召回性能。作者对方法的局限性进行了坦诚声明。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are highly effective in retrieving semantically relevant images. However, in practice, relevance alone is often insufficient. Systems must also achieve Result Diversification (RD) across composite attributes such as geography and time, a task for which precise control remains challenging. Current re-ranking methods, such as Multi-Source Determinantal Point Processes (MS-DPP), address this using manifold-based repulsion over similarity representations. Although this strategy is effective for broad exploration, it exposes a key limitation in manifold-based models: when subjected to diversity-decrease tasks on discrete metadata, they suffer substantial degradation in early-rank recall.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce MASCOT (Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval). Instead of relying on manifold repulsion, MASCOT formulates multi-attribute diversity as a resource allocation problem, projecting attributes into a soft-binning space weighted by query-driven importance. Averaged across the three PixelProse diversity-decrease tasks, MASCOT preserves an early-rank recall (R@10) of 88.58%, while MS-DPP retains 67.63%. The margin widens under composite constraints: on PP_geo_hour, where temporal and geographic diversity must be suppressed simultaneously, MS-DPP's recall collapses from 0.9737 to 0.4931 and its top-ranked result degrades to R@1 = 0.23, while MASCOT holds R@10 = 0.9410 and R@1 = 0.7202 at a diversity metric above the unconstrained baseline. We do not claim uniform superiority: on aggregate diversity-relevance scores our own simpler ablations attain higher harmonic means on all three decrease tasks, and MASCOT's advantage is specific to recall beyond rank 1 under composite constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI领域缺乏对推理的统一定义，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者基于文献综合提出操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供AI推理研究交流的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的课题之一，但生成式AI社区对推理缺乏统一定义，常隐含拒绝逻辑学和可验证自动推理的历史处理方式。这种定义模糊性使推理评估的构念效度不可验证，损害了可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Method: 本文是一篇立场性（position）论文，通过综合已有文献提出操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；同时制定了一套人工智能推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单（checklist）。

Result: 提供了两点成果：(1)基于文献综合的操作性定义；(2)AI推理研究交流的最佳实践清单。这些工具旨在解决定义模糊导致的构念效度问题。

Conclusion: 推理的定义模糊性是可以通过明确的操作性定义和沟通规范来解决的。作者呼吁生成式AI社区重视逻辑和可验证自动推理的历史传统，以建立可验证的可信自主推理评估体系。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [169] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力，发现前沿模型在高压下约1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益被部署为共同科学家，但在制度压力下其维护科研诚信的能力尚未被系统测量，现有评估缺乏对这一关键维度的覆盖。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议，评估模型在不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于证据的决策三个维度上的表现。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型在约1/3的诚信关键决策上失败；显式压力导致模型顺从不当行为，隐式语境重构则引发对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；分类准确率低的模型在基于证据的决策上反而表现更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度在结构上相互分离。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能在表面上有帮助性的同时隐藏诚信缺陷，产生两类部署风险：助长科研不当行为和削弱对AI辅助研究的信任，正确伦理行动不依赖于准确分类。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [170] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证现代AI对齐方法虽然是设计用于防止有害输出的，但本质上是一种双重用途技术，容易被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操纵。作者呼吁社区在AI被广泛采用为信息提供者的背景下，正视这一双重用途潜力并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI作为信息提供者被快速采用、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境趋于威权化，AI对齐技术的滥用风险日益增大。作者认为现在必须讨论这一双重用途潜力，以避免其被用作信息主导权的工具。

Method: 通过系统性地将当前AI对齐技术及其技术可能性映射到实际的滥用案例中，论证"完美对齐"模型的追求在无意中也提供了恶意行为者不断改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 本文未提供实证实验结果，而是通过概念映射和案例分析，展示了AI对齐机制从安全工具转向审查与操控工具的双重用途性质及其现实风险。

Conclusion: 作者敦促AI社区考虑AI对齐机制的故意滥用，并提出缓解策略以防范这一双重用途潜力，同时强调需要尽早采取行动以应对不断加剧的社会政治风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [171] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个包含500个ETHICS基准条目的道德判断基准，对比人类标注者与LLM在最终标签和支撑理由两个层面的一致性，发现模型在标签层面与人类高度一致，但在道德理由层面（如伤害、尊重、守信、公正等类别的权重分配）存在系统性分歧，证明标签一致性不等于真正的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐评估常以与人类判断的一致性作为代理指标，但最终标签一致并不能证明模型与人类基于相同的道德原则进行推理。两个智能体可能得出相同结论却依据完全不同的理由，因此需要区分"判断一致"与"理由一致"。

Method: 构建一个基于ETHICS的500项目基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，同时收集人类标注者和多种LLM（涵盖前沿模型与开源模型）的最终标签及支持性理由标注，然后进行标签层面的同意率分析和理由层面的道德原则分配分析。

Result: 模型与人类标注者多数标签的同意率通常很高，但理由层面的分析揭示了系统性分歧：模型在伤害、尊重、守信、公正、应得和借口相关性等道德类别上的注意力分配与人类不同，即使最终标签一致时也是如此。

Conclusion: 同意（agreement）不应被视为等同于对齐（alignment）。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安心感，必须辅以对模型判断中理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MAS-DecStream系统，其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP协议——一种扩展的合同网协议，结合语义CFP制定、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，用于异构移动边缘-云基础设施中的去中心化调度。实验表明该协议将延迟违规率降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，冲突解决率达0.91，相比多轮基于规则的基线效用提升最高22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中运行时，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格的QoS要求，使得去中心化调度变得复杂困难，需要更智能的调度机制来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP协议，扩展经典合同网协议，加入语义CFP制定、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证等机制。边缘集群代理基于本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文提炼自然语言卸载提案，同时确保硬性资源和QoS约束保持确定性。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验在三个层面评估该扩展：单轮与多轮CNP对比、基于规则与LLM辅助细化对比、固定模型单轮与多轮协商对比。结果显示延迟违规率降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用比多轮基于规则的基线提高最高22%。另25个案例评估显示模型和提示词相关的准确度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP细化是协议层面的主要收益来源，LLM辅助在定性和不确定运行时上下文处理方面增加价值，为大规模边缘-云流处理调度提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [173] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统需要具备"认知对齐"能力——即以与用户相似的思维方式进行推理，并忠实传达其推理过程。文章通过证据综述和新调查数据论证认知对齐对可理解性和信任度的重要性，并提出了实现认知对齐的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者被使用，现有对齐方法主要关注价值观对齐，但忽视了认知层面的对齐。作者认为认知错位是AI在众多场景中被采纳的主要障碍，因此需要系统地阐述这一问题并推动相关研究。

Method: 本文采用综述与实证相结合的方法：首先回顾认知对齐如何提升可理解性和可信度的已有证据；其次通过新的调查数据展示用户在何种情境下认为认知对齐"必不可少"；最后分析现有对齐方法的不足，提出研究议程来填补这些空白。

Result: 调查数据显示，当AI的判断或行动理由对用户很重要时，许多用户认为认知对齐是"必不可少的"。文章识别出现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距，指出这些差距可能阻碍AI在诸多设想场景中的应用。

Conclusion: 认知错位是AI采纳的重要障碍，实现认知对齐对于创建用户既愿意依赖又有理由信赖的AI系统至关重要。文章呼吁开展研究以弥合现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [174] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了六家提供商的九个大语言模型，发现提示词的语言可以改变模型在高风险场景（核武器国家是否攻击）中的决策。日语提示显著降低了Claude系列和Gemini Pro 3.1的发射率，机制是模型被要求用日语推理时自发产生道德词汇，而仅输入为日语、推理为英语则无效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多用于战略和咨询场景，但安全性评估通常仅用英语进行。作者质疑以英语为中心的评估可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和保障机制，因此系统性地检验语言是否会影响模型在高赌注场景中的决策。

Method: 设计单轮博弈论情境，模型被要求向核武器国家提供是否攻击无防御对手的建议。提示词故意非道德化且跨语言策略上完全相同，测试九种模型，并设计跨语言实验（英语提示但要求日语推理）以隔离机制。

Result: 日语提示使Claude Sonnet 4.6发射率从40%降至0%（非必要打击场景）和从93%降至17%（争议场景），在策略理性打击时影响最小。Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验显示，用日语推理时发射率从93%降至37%，而输入语言非决定因素。日语推理时模型自发产生提示中完全缺失的道德词汇。五种其他模型无语言效应，但几乎所有条件下都发射。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中存在的风险和保障。语言效应依赖于模型在英语中已有犹豫倾向，说明安全对齐需跨语言评估。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [175] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dual-Flow Transformer架构，通过将推理计算分为主流（处理prompt并写入KV缓存）和辅助流（仅从最后一个prompt位置开始激活，用于续写预测），实现了计算资源在prefill和decode两个推理阶段之间的灵活分配，从而在不增加prefill成本的情况下提升续写质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推理请求量增加，累积推理成本变得比一次性训练成本更重要。prefill阶段并行且计算受限，decode阶段串行且受内存带宽限制，传统宽度或深度扩展会同时增加两个阶段的成本，因为每个新增层在两个阶段都会被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算只分配给续写预测，同时保持prompt处理的主计算不变。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流是完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最终prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测计算但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两个流共享主要的注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主流和辅助流的专家扇出可以独立控制，分别影响prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在与token数匹配的比较中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下取得了更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，作者研究了两种场景：在固定prefill专家计算时增加decode计算，以及将固定decode专家预算在两个流之间重新分配。实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer通过将计算分配从统一的层扩展解耦为分阶段的、可独立控制的策略，为优化大模型推理成本与质量的平衡提供了新思路，特别是在MoE模型中展现出对prefill和decode计算进行独立调优的可能性。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases.
